<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前作建立了奇点的数学定义、分类和存在性证明框架后，本文旨在研究空间均匀宇宙学模型中时空奇点的具体实现形式。尽管空间均匀性是一个强对称性假设，但这类模型仍展现出意外复杂的奇点行为，需要系统梳理其几何基础、场方程解、奇点分类以及奇异边界的层次结构，为后续动力学分析奠定基础。

Method: 文章采用几何-动力学分析方法：首先回顾空间均匀性的几何基础和Bianchi分类体系；推导正交和倾斜完美流体宇宙学的场方程；以Kasner解和Bianchi I解作为各向异性奇异行为的基本原型；利用Raychaudhuri聚焦定理给出均匀Cauchy发展达到有限固有时间边界的一般条件；区分物质奇点（密度和Ricci曲率发散）与非标量奇点或whimper奇点（与Cauchy视界和极端流体倾斜相关）；重新审视经典Ellis-King分析框架，结合后续发展包括Bianchi VIII/IX模型的曲率爆破严格结果、测地完备倾斜时空中的运动学奇点以及偶极宇宙学中whimper行为的重新出现。

Result: 研究表明，即使在空间均匀的宇宙学模型中，也存在极其丰富的奇异边界层次结构。具体包括：通过Raychaudhuri方程确立了均匀模型中奇点形成的普遍条件；证实了Bianchi VIII和IX模型中存在曲率爆破的严格结果；发现了测地完备的倾斜时空中仍可出现运动学奇点；识别出偶极宇宙学中whimper奇点的重新出现。这些结果揭示了从经典物质奇点到更微妙的非标量奇点和whimper奇点的完整谱系。

Conclusion: 空间均匀宇宙学模型尽管具有高度对称性，但展现出异常丰富的奇点现象学。文章建立了从几何基础、场方程、原型解到奇点分类的完整框架，揭示了物质奇点、Cauchy视界奇点、运动学奇点和whimper奇点等多层次奇异结构。这为理解广义相对论中时空奇点的本质提供了重要案例研究，同时为后续探讨这些奇异边界的详细动力学行为（哈密顿宇宙学、Kasner跃迁、Mixmaster动力学和BKL猜想）奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This article is the second part of a review devoted to spacetime singularities in classical General Relativity. While Primer I developed the mathematical framework for defining, classifying, and proving the existence of singularities, the present work examines their concrete realization in spatially homogeneous cosmological models. After reviewing the geometrical foundations of homogeneity and the Bianchi classification, we derive the field equations for orthogonal and tilted perfect fluid cosmologies and use the Kasner and Bianchi I solutions as elementary prototypes of anisotropic singular behaviour. Raychaudhuri focusing provides general conditions under which the homogeneous Cauchy development reaches a finite proper time boundary. We then distinguish matter singularities, characterized by divergent density and Ricci curvature, from more subtle non-scalar or whimper singularities associated with Cauchy horizons and extreme fluid tilt. The classical Ellis-King analysis is reconsidered in the light of later developments, including rigorous curvature-blow-up results for Bianchi VIII and IX models, kinematic singularities in geodesically complete tilted spacetimes, and the recent reappearance of whimper behaviour in dipole cosmology. The resulting picture shows that even spatially homogeneous cosmologies exhibit a remarkably rich hierarchy of singular boundaries. The detailed dynamical approach to these boundaries - Hamiltonian cosmology, Kasner transitions, Mixmaster dynamics and the BKL conjecture - is reserved for Primer III.

</details>


### [100] [Generalized Regular Black Holes with Tunable Cores and Geodesic Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25113)
*Hassan Hassanabadi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类由核心指数n、正则化尺度a和无量纲形变参数η刻画的静态球对称正则黑洞几何族,通过可调的有效密度分布构造,包含Bardeen几何作为特殊情况,并系统研究了其视界结构、热力学性质、零测地线(光子球、阴影半径)、弱引力透镜以及类时测地线运动等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统奇点黑洞在中心存在物理发散问题,需要构造正则黑洞模型来避免中心奇点;现有正则黑洞模型(如Bardeen黑洞)缺乏参数灵活性和统一框架;需要系统研究可调正则核心和类Reissner-Nordström外部形变如何共同影响黑洞的热力学和测地线性质。

Method: 从正的归一化有效密度分布出发,通过离散核心指数n控制中心密度衰减阶数(n最低时为de Sitter型核心,n增大时趋向类Minkowski中心);引入正则化尺度a和形变参数η,η≠0时产生类Reissner-Nordström渐近修正;推导视界结构、极端性条件、Hawking温度、熵及热力学恒等式;分析零测地线获得光子球、临界碰撞参数和阴影半径,包括弱形变近似和精确极限情况;计算光子轨道角频率和Lyapunov指数以分离轨道和不稳定性时间尺度;通过渐近度规的扇区隔离展开研究弱引力透镜和放大率;研究类时测地线、圆轨道运动、临界束缚轨道和最内稳定圆轨道,给出精确隐式关系和微扰表达式。

Result: 成功构造了包含Bardeen几何和Schwarzschild几何为特殊情况的正则黑洞族;获得了完整的视界结构和热力学性质(温度、熵及其满足的热力学恒等式);给出了光子球、阴影半径的解析弱形变近似和精确表达式,并与Reissner-Nordström行为比较;通过光子轨道频率和Lyapunov指数的比值提供了阴影半径之外的额外信息;导出了圆轨道、临界束缚轨道和最内稳定圆轨道的精确隐式关系和微扰表达式;完成了弱引力透镜效应的分析。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个统一框架,用于系统研究可调正则核心和类RN外部形变对正则黑洞热力学和测地线性质的影响;通过核心指数n可以灵活控制中心区域从de Sitter型到类Minkowski型的过渡,形变参数η则引入渐近修正;模型成功避免了中心奇点问题,同时保持了足够的参数灵活性以描述不同物理情形;零测地线和类时测地线的完整分析为观测特征(阴影、透镜、粒子轨道)提供了理论预测,有助于通过天文观测区分不同正则黑洞模型。

Abstract: We introduce a family of static and spherically symmetric regular black-hole geometries characterized by a discrete core index $n$, a regularization scale $a$, and a dimensionless deformation parameter $η$. The construction starts from a positive, normalized effective density profile with a tunable central behavior. The lowest member contains the Bardeen geometry as a limiting case, whereas a nonzero deformation parameter generates a Reissner--Nordström-like asymptotic correction. The parameter $n$ controls the order of central depletion: the lowest member possesses a de Sitter-type core, whereas higher members approach a Minkowski-like center with vanishing density and curvature. We determine the horizon structure, extremality condition, Hawking temperature, entropy, and the horizon thermodynamic identity implied by the radial Einstein equation. Null geodesics are analyzed to obtain the photon sphere, critical impact parameter, and shadow radius, including analytic weak-deformation approximations, the exact Schwarzschild and Bardeen limits, and comparison with the leading Reissner--Nordström-like behavior. The photon-orbit angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent are then used to separate orbital and instability timescales; their ratio provides information not already contained in the shadow radius. Weak gravitational lensing and magnification are discussed through a controlled sector-isolated expansion of the asymptotic metric. We further study timelike geodesics, circular motion, the marginally bound orbit, and the innermost stable circular orbit, deriving exact implicit relations and perturbative expressions. The model provides a unified framework for investigating how a tunable regular core and an RN-like exterior deformation affect the thermodynamic and geodesic properties of regular black holes.

</details>


### [101] [Event Detection and Finite-Grid Effects at an Imposed Absorbing Boundary in Newtonian Black Hole Orbit Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25137)
*Gregory M Davis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了数值模拟中吸收边界的离散评估方式如何影响黑洞轨道分类，发现表观临界近心点距离取决于边界设置及其数值实现方法，强调需要区分数值效应与真实动力学阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑洞轨道的数值分类中，内部捕获边界或吸收边界的实现方式会影响分类结果。现有研究缺乏对边界离散评估方式如何系统性影响轨道分类的深入分析，需要明确数值效应与物理动力学阈值之间的区别。

Method: 采用开普勒系统作为测试平台，在孤立牛顿点质量周围积分无质量粒子轨道。粒子在近心点初始化，设置吸收边界为rsink = 10^4 RS（史瓦西半径）。由于开普勒近心点距离q保持恒定，可以解析确定预期分类结果。对比了在初始化时刻（t=0）评估边界条件与忽略初始化评估两种方案，并在不同网格分辨率下测试分类准确性。

Result: 忽略初始化时刻边界评估的分类器会遗漏已满足移除条件的粒子，导致分类错误。在t=0时刻进行边界评估可在所有采样网格上准确重现解析分类结果。随着网格分辨率提高，推断的临界近心点距离逐渐趋近于设定的吸收边界半径。

Conclusion: 研究证明表观临界近心点距离同时依赖于施加的吸收边界位置及其数值评估方式。数值实现细节会显著影响轨道分类结果，因此在黑洞轨道研究中必须严格区分数值效应与真实的动力学阈值，避免将数值人工产物误认为物理现象。该结果对黑洞吸积盘模拟和引力波源轨道演化等研究具有重要方法学意义。

Abstract: Numerical classification of black-hole orbits can depend on how prescribed inner capture or absorbing boundaries are implemented. This study investigated how discrete evaluation of such a boundary affects orbit classification. Massless particles were integrated about an isolated Newtonian point mass and initialized at periapsis with an absorbing boundary at rsink = 104RS. Because the Keplerian periapsis q is constant, the expected classification can be analytically determined. A classifier omitting evaluation at initialization can miss particles that already satisfy the removal criterion, but evaluation at t = 0 reproduced the analytic classification on every sampled grid. As the grid resolution increased, the inferred critical periapsis approached the imposed sink radius. These results demonstrate that an apparent critical periapsis can depend on both the imposed boundary and its numerical evaluation, emphasizing the need to distinguish numerical effects from genuine dynamical thresholds.

</details>


### [102] [The BTZ black hole, the Third Law and gravitational collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25218)
*Aidan M. McSharry,Harvey S. Reall,Jorge E. Santos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了BTZ黑洞的热力学第三定律，表明包含俘获面的初始数据不能在有限时间内演化为事件视界上与极端BTZ重合的黑洞。尽管如此，无质量标量场的引力坍缩可以在有限时间内形成极端BTZ黑洞，这种差异源于引力坍缩发生在Neveu-Schwarz扇区，而极端BTZ在Ramond扇区中具有超对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞热力学第三定律在一般情况下的适用性尚不明确。本研究旨在验证该定律是否适用于三维引力中的BTZ黑洞，特别是在耦合满足主能量条件的物质时，探讨包含俘获面的初始数据能否在有限时间内演化为极端黑洞，以及不同物理扇区对这一过程的影响。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：1）定义准局域能量和角动量；2）使用旋量方法建立BPS不等式，并证明该不等式被极端BTZ解饱和；3）分析无质量标量场的引力坍缩过程；4）区分Neveu-Schwarz扇区和Ramond扇区中的物理行为；5）研究沿事件视界在无限时间内趋于极端BTZ的解。

Result: 研究得到以下主要结果：1）证明了BTZ黑洞满足热力学第三定律——包含俘获面的初始数据不能在有限时间内演化为事件视界上与极端BTZ重合的黑洞，该结论对任意无穷远边界条件成立；2）发现无质量标量场的引力坍缩可以在有限时间内形成极端BTZ黑洞；3）揭示了这种表面矛盾的根源：引力坍缩发生在Neveu-Schwarz扇区，而极端BTZ在Ramond扇区中具有超对称性；4）讨论了沿事件视界在无限时间内趋于极端BTZ的解的存在性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功证明了BTZ黑洞的热力学第三定律，确认包含俘获面的初始数据无法在有限时间内演化为极端黑洞。然而，无质量标量场的引力坍缩仍可在有限时间内形成极端BTZ黑洞，这一看似矛盾的现象通过区分不同的物理扇区得到解释：热力学第三定律适用于通常的Neveu-Schwarz扇区，而极端BTZ的超对称性质属于Ramond扇区。这一结果深化了对黑洞热力学定律、超对称性与引力坍缩之间关系的理解，为三维引力理论提供了重要的理论约束。

Abstract: We prove that a Third Law of black hole mechanics holds for the BTZ black hole in 3d gravity coupled to matter obeying the dominant energy condition. This law states that initial data containing a trapped surface cannot evolve in finite time to a black hole that coincides with extremal BTZ on the event horizon, for any choice of boundary conditions at infinity. The result is proved by defining a quasilocal energy and angular momentum and using spinorial methods to establish a BPS inequality that is saturated by the extremal BTZ solution. Nevertheless, we show that gravitational collapse of a massless scalar field can result in the formation of an extremal BTZ black hole in finite time. The difference between these results arises because gravitational collapse occurs in the Neveu-Schwarz sector, whereas extremal BTZ is supersymmetric in the Ramond sector. Solutions that settle down to extremal BTZ in infinite time along the event horizon are also discussed.

</details>


### [103] [Gravity-Informed Neural Networks for Post-Newtonian Binary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25222)
*Gabriele Barbagallo,Javier Matulich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种引力信息神经网络（GravINNs）框架，用于学习后牛顿双星轨道动力学。该方法将运动方程直接整合到训练过程中，在保守动力学和耗散动力学两种情况下均能准确模拟轨道演化，无需重复数值积分，并已开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的后牛顿双星轨道动力学模拟需要重复的数值积分计算，计算成本高昂且缺乏灵活性。为了解决这一问题，研究者希望利用物理信息神经网络（PINNs）构建一种高效、准确且灵活的替代方法，能够直接将物理运动方程嵌入到神经网络训练中，从而在保守和耗散两种动力学情形下都能快速准确地预测轨道演化。

Method: 本文在三个互补层次上开发了GravINNs框架：（1）构建单轨道代理模型用于保守后牛顿动力学，并评估其在不同轨道配置下的准确性；（2）将方法扩展到参数化模型，跨越由初始分离距离、径向和切向动量以及对称质量比定义的四维参数空间，使单一训练网络能够表示整个轨道族，替代重复的数值积分；（3）考虑耗散动力学，纳入2.5阶后牛顿的主导辐射反作用贡献，在训练中不使用耗散参考轨迹，仅通过将运动方程直接整合到损失函数中来实现学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，GravINNs在保守和耗散后牛顿动力学中均能提供准确的预测。在保守情况下，单轨道和参数化模型在不同轨道配置上都表现出高精度。在耗散情况下，网络在不使用耗散参考轨迹的情况下，准确再现了轨道演化和相关的长期能量损失。完整的实现代码和模型已通过GravINNs仓库公开发布。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了物理信息神经网络（PINNs）能够为保守和耗散后牛顿动力学提供准确且灵活的表示方法。GravINNs框架成功地将引力物理方程嵌入神经网络训练，实现了高效的双星轨道动力学建模，避免了重复数值积分的计算负担，为引力波天文学和相关领域的快速模拟提供了有前景的工具。

Abstract: We introduce a gravity-informed neural-network (GravINNs) framework for learning post-Newtonian binary orbital dynamics. The method incorporates the equations of motion directly into the training phase and is developed at three complementary levels. First, we construct single-orbit surrogates for conservative post-Newtonian dynamics and assess their accuracy across different orbital configurations. We then extend the approach to a parametric model spanning a four-dimensional parameter space defined by the initial separation, radial and tangential momenta, and symmetric mass ratio, allowing a single trained network to represent entire families of orbits and replace repeated numerical integrations. Finally, we consider dissipative dynamics by including the leading radiation-reaction contribution at $2.5$PN order. In this regime, the network accurately reproduces both the orbital evolution and the associated secular energy loss without using dissipative reference trajectories in the training. These results show that physics-informed neural-networks (PINNs) can provide accurate and flexible representations of both conservative and dissipative post-Newtonian dynamics. The complete implementation and the models used in this work are publicly available through the \texttt{GravINNs} repository.

</details>


### [104] [Gravitational wave signatures of subsolar neutron star formation in collapsar disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25225)
*Vishal Baibhav,Valerio De Luca,Loris Del Grosso,Emanuele Berti,Brian D. Metzger,Lam Hui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了collapsar模型中亚太阳质量中子星双星系统的引力波特征，这些系统在大质量快速旋转恒星坍缩形成的黑洞吸积盘中产生，并通过多普勒调制、气体驱动迁移和潮汐相互作用等环境印记形成独特的引力波信号，可与其他亚太阳质量并合情景区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准恒星核坍缩模型预测会产生质量超过太阳质量的中子星，但collapsar模型中的吸积盘外围区域可能碎裂形成亚太阳质量中子星。这些特殊天体的并合会产生独特的引力波信号，需要系统研究其特征以便探测器识别和验证collapsar形成路径。

Method: 本文通过理论建模分析了collapsar环境中亚太阳质量中子星双星系统的引力波特征，重点关注三个关键环境效应：（1）围绕中心黑洞轨道运动产生的多普勒效应和其他相位调制；（2）吸积盘中的气体驱动迁移；（3）并合螺旋过程中的潮汐相互作用。通过这些环境印记与异常低的成分质量相结合，构建了独特的波形特征模型。

Result: 研究表明，亚太阳质量成分与collapsar环境印记（多普勒调制、气体迁移、潮汐效应）的组合产生了独特的引力波波形特征，这些特征可以将collapsar通道与其他亚太阳质量并合情景明确区分开来。当前和下一代引力波探测器具有良好的能力来识别此类天体的内禀性质。

Conclusion: collapsar模型中形成的亚太阳质量中子星双星系统具有可识别的独特引力波特征。如果未来探测到亚太阳质量并合事件，当前和下一代引力波探测器有望通过这些特征对collapsar形成路径进行检验和约束，为理解这一特殊天体物理过程提供观测证据。

Abstract: Standard models for stellar core collapse predict the birth of neutron stars with masses exceeding a solar mass. In the collapsar model, a rapidly rotating massive star collapses to a black hole surrounded by a dense, neutrino-cooled accretion disk, whose outer regions may in some cases fragment into neutron-rich clumps that collapse to form subsolar-mass neutron stars. These disk-formed objects can then merge either with one another or, following possibly hierarchical evolution, with the central black hole. In this work, we characterize the gravitational-wave signatures of these binaries, focusing on distinctive features of the collapsar environment: Doppler and other phase modulations from orbital motion about the central black hole, gas-driven migration in the disk, and tidal interactions during inspiral. We show that the combination of anomalously low component masses with these environmental imprints produces unique waveform signatures that can distinguish the collapsar channel from other subsolar merger scenarios. Current and next-generation detectors have good prospects for identifying the intrinsic properties of such objects and, if subsolar mergers are discovered, for testing and constraining the collapsar formation pathway.

</details>


### [105] [Accurate Black Hole Quasinormal Modes, Regge Poles, and Greybody Factors from Light Ring Ladder Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25228)
*Rajes Ghosh,David Pereñiguez,Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了黑洞扰动方程中隐藏的阶梯对称性,将准正规模、Regge极点和灰体因子的计算映射为非谐振子的量子力学问题,为高频regime下球对称黑洞响应提供了系统高效的计算框架,并在史瓦西黑洞中获得了高精度解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞的特征振荡与其不稳定光环附近的捕获零测地线相关,在高频极限下这种对应关系变得精确。然而,现有的近似方案在计算黑洞响应(准正规模、Regge极点、灰体因子)时存在收敛性问题。因此需要发展一个更系统、更高效且具有更好收敛性质的理论框架来计算球对称黑洞的响应特性。

Method: 在高频regime下,作者发现黑洞扰动方程中存在隐藏的阶梯对称性(ladder symmetry)。利用这一对称性,将球对称黑洞的准正规模、Regge极点和灰体因子的计算问题映射到非谐振子(anharmonic oscillator)的量子力学问题上。通过这种映射关系,对史瓦西黑洞进行了角动量倒数的高阶解析展开(至18阶),并探索了不同的重求和方案(resummation schemes)以进一步提高计算精度。

Result: 对于史瓦西黑洞,成功通过角动量倒数展开至18阶,解析计算了准正规模、Regge极点和灰体因子这三个物理量,获得了高精度结果。与现有近似方案相比,该方法展现出改进的收敛性质。通过不同的重求和方案,进一步提升了Regge极点的精度,特别是显著提高了灰体因子的计算准确度。

Conclusion: 通过揭示黑洞扰动方程中的隐藏阶梯对称性,建立了从黑洞物理到量子谐振子问题的精确映射,为高频regime下黑洞响应的计算提供了系统、高效且高精度的理论框架。该框架不仅在史瓦西黑洞中得到验证,还具有良好的可扩展性,可以直接推广应用于旋转黑洞和修正引力理论,为黑洞物理和引力波天文学研究提供了强有力的理论工具。

Abstract: The characteristic oscillations of a black hole can be understood in terms of null rays trapped near its unstable light ring, with this correspondence becoming exact in the high-frequency limit. In this regime, we uncover a hidden ladder symmetry in the perturbation equations, which maps the calculation of quasinormal modes, Regge poles, and greybody factors for a spherically symmetric black hole onto the quantum-mechanical problem of an anharmonic oscillator. This provides a systematic, efficient framework for computing the black hole response, with improved convergence properties compared with existing approximation schemes. As an explicit application, we analytically compute all three quantities for a Schwarzschild black hole through eighteenth order in inverse angular momentum, obtaining highly accurate results. We explore different resummation schemes and find that they can further improve the accuracy of the Regge poles and, particularly, the greybody factors. Our framework can be readily extended to rotating black holes and to modified theories of gravity.

</details>


### [106] [Higher derivative Hořava-Lifshitz fermions: from superconductivity to atomic spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25236)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,K. E. L. de Farias,N. Heidari,M. Paganelly,A. F. Santos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有六维空间导数算符的各向同性Hořava-Lifshitz费米子理论(z=2扇区),系统分析了修正色散关系对超导配对、散射过程和氢原子能级的影响,为通过光谱学实验约束该理论提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hořava-Lifshitz引力理论作为量子引力的候选理论,其费米子扇区的物理效应尚未充分研究。特别是高维空间导数算符如何在保持一阶时间演化的同时修改紫外色散关系,以及这种修正在凝聚态物理(超导配对)、散射理论和原子光谱学中的可观测后果,需要系统的理论分析来建立实验约束。

Method: 研究采用三种互补方法探讨z=2扇区的物理后果:(1)使用BCS/NJL平均场理论处理费米子配对,推导粒子和反粒子准粒子谱、有限温度能隙方程及稳定超导相的规范不变判据;(2)以标量极点分母作为最小自旋无关交换核,导出指数阻尼振荡型有质量势,并计算相应的玻恩散射振幅;(3)以闭合形式计算氢原子1S、2S和2P能级的诱导位移,保留相互作用程度的完全依赖性。

Result: 修正色散关系显著改变了控制配对尺度的态密度和迈斯纳响应中的电磁顶点。导出的势函数具有指数阻尼和振荡特性,其四次动量依赖使前向和总散射截面均保持有限,并在大动量转移区域产生与卢瑟福散射明显不同的行为。氢原子能级位移的闭合形式解析结果直接给出了核子和轻子耦合常数乘积的光谱学约束,μ子原子对短程Hořava-Lifshitz扇区表现出相应的敏感性。

Conclusion: 六维空间导数算符修正的Hořava-Lifshitz费米子理论在超导物理、散射过程和原子光谱学中产生可观测的特征性效应。氢原子(特别是μ子原子)能级的精密光谱测量可以为该理论的短程耦合参数提供直接实验约束,为检验Hořava-Lifshitz引力理论的费米子扇区开辟了光谱学途径。

Abstract: We investigate an isotropic Hořava-Lifshitz fermion theory supplemented by a dimension-six spatial derivative operator, preserving first order time evolution while modifying the ultraviolet dispersion relation. Within the regime of validity of the derivative expansion, we develop three complementary consequences of the $z=2$ sector. First, a Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer/Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (BCS/NJL) mean field treatment of fermion pairing yields the particle and antiparticle quasiparticle spectra, the finite temperature gap equation, and the gauge invariant criterion for a stable superconducting phase. The modified dispersion alters both the density of states governing the pairing scale and the electromagnetic vertices entering the Meissner response. Second, using the scalar pole denominator as a minimal spin independent exchange kernel, we derive an exponentially damped and oscillatory massive potential and obtain the corresponding Born scattering amplitudes. The quartic momentum dependence renders both the forward and total cross sections finite and produces a large transfer behavior distinct from Rutherford scattering. Finally, we evaluate the induced shifts of the hydrogenic $1S$, $2S$, and $2P$ levels in closed form, retaining the full dependence on the interaction range. In other words, these latter results gives direct spectroscopic constraints on the product of nuclear and leptonic couplings, with muonic atoms, showing the respective sensitivity to the short distance Hořava-Lifshitz sector.

</details>


### [107] [Frozen-In Gravitational Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25240)
*Felipe A. Asenjo,Maricarmen A. Winkler,Luca Comisso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过类比非线性电动力学框架重新表述爱因斯坦场方程,发现广义相对论中存在引力场"冻结"现象、守恒的"引力磁"通量和引力螺旋度,为时空非线性动力学提供了拓扑约束和组织原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空在爱因斯坦场方程支配下可经历复杂的非线性演化,核心挑战是理解演化过程中产生、持续存在并相互作用的几何结构。现有理论框架缺乏对时空动力学中拓扑性质和场线连通性的系统性理解。

Method: 采用类似连续介质非线性电动力学的形式来重新表述爱因斯坦方程,引入引力场的理想欧姆型条件,通过这一框架分析引力场的连通性、通量守恒和拓扑性质,建立引力场线结构的数学描述。

Result: 证明了广义相对论允许引力场连接(gravitational field connections)的存在——即由时空动力学维持连通性的二维曲面及相关场线,发现了"冻结"行为(frozen-in behavior);导出了守恒的"引力磁"通量;发现了具有明确拓扑解释的守恒引力螺旋度(gravitational helicity),它与引力场线结构直接相关。

Conclusion: 研究识别出了时空演化所必须满足的明确拓扑约束,为理解时空的非线性动力学提供了一个组织原理。引力场的"冻结"行为、守恒通量和螺旋度共同构成了描述时空几何演化的拓扑框架,揭示了广义相对论中深层的几何-拓扑结构。

Abstract: Spacetime can undergo complex nonlinear evolution, as governed by the Einstein field equations, and a central challenge is to understand the geometric structures that arise, persist, and interact throughout its evolution. Using a formulation of Einstein equations that parallels nonlinear electrodynamics of continuous media, we show that general relativity admits gravitational field connections---two-surfaces and associated field lines whose connectivity is maintained by the spacetime dynamics. This gravitational frozen-in behavior is enabled by an ideal Ohm-type condition for the gravitational field. We further show that the same framework naturally leads to a conserved ``gravitational magnetic'' flux. A conserved gravitational helicity also emerges, with a clear topological interpretation in terms of gravitational field-line structures. These results identify well-defined topological constraints on admissible spacetime evolution and provide an organizing principle underlying the nonlinear dynamics of spacetime.

</details>


### [108] [Reconnection of Gravitational Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25241)
*Luca Comisso,Felipe A. Asenjo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四标架广义相对论框架下发展了引力重联理论,研究引力场片连通性变化及其对能量-动量交换和引力螺度演化的影响,为动态时空中引力连通性变化提供了定量描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在四标架表述的广义相对论中,理想欧姆型条件要求引力通量守恒和引力场片连通性保持。然而,当偏离理想引力演化时,引力场片的连通性可能发生改变。需要发展一套理论来定量描述这种非理想演化过程——即引力重联现象,以及它对引力场和物质场之间能量-动量交换的影响。

Method: 作者采用四标架(tetrad)表述的广义相对论框架,通过研究引力场冻结演化条件的破缺来定义引力重联。具体方法包括:1) 建立引力场片连通性变化的数学描述;2) 推导引力重联率的定量度量指标;3) 分析引力重联对引力扇区与物质扇区之间能量-动量交换的影响;4) 确定引力重联如何修正引力螺度(gravitational helicity)的演化方程。

Result: 研究获得了以下主要结果:1) 给出了引力重联率的定量度量方法;2) 证明了引力重联会改变引力扇区和物质扇区之间的能量-动量交换过程;3) 确定了引力重联对引力螺度演化的修正效应;4) 建立了描述动态时空中引力连通性变化及其物理后果的完整定量框架。

Conclusion: 本文成功发展了引力重联理论,为描述动态时空中引力场片连通性变化提供了严格的定量框架。引力重联现象不仅改变引力场的拓扑结构,还会影响引力与物质之间的能量-动量交换以及引力螺度的演化。这一理论框架为理解强引力场动力学过程中的非理想演化效应提供了新的分析工具,可能对黑洞并合、中子星碰撞等极端引力事件的研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: In a tetrad formulation of general relativity, an ideal Ohm-type condition for the gravitational field implies the conservation of gravitational fluxes and the preservation of the connectivity of gravitational field sheets. We investigate the consequences of departures from ideal gravitational evolution by developing a theory of gravitational reconnection, defined as a change in the connectivity of gravitational field sheets arising from the breakdown of the ideal frozen-in evolution of the gravitational field. We derive quantitative measures of the gravitational reconnection rate, show that gravitational reconnection alters the energy-momentum exchange between the gravitational and matter sectors, and determine how it modifies the evolution of gravitational helicity. Our results provide a quantitative framework for describing changes in gravitational connectivity and their consequences in dynamical spacetimes.

</details>


### [109] [Kerr-Degenerate Shadows and Distinct Strong-Deflection Lensing in Rotating Hayward-like and Bardeen-like Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25258)
*Chen-Hung Hsiao,Limei Yuan,Yidun Wan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在统一的类Kerr轴对称框架下研究了旋转Hayward型和Bardeen型正则黑洞几何，通过面积半径函数区分模型。研究发现两种几何具有相同的渐近Komar电荷和光子阴影边界（与相同参数的Kerr黑洞一致），但在强偏转极限下的某些观测量上存在差异，未来对时间延迟、像分离和相对亮度的精密测量有望区分这些模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kerr黑洞在环状奇点处存在曲率发散问题。为了构建物理上更合理的正则黑洞模型，需要研究旋转的正则几何（如Hayward型和Bardeen型），并系统比较它们与Kerr黑洞在观测特征上的异同，特别是在事件视界望远镜（EHT）等观测手段日益精确的背景下，探索是否能通过观测约束或区分这些理论模型。

Method: 采用统一的类Kerr轴对称度规ansatz，通过不同的面积半径函数R_i(ρ)来区分Hayward型和Bardeen型几何。分析了视界结构的参数域分布、Komar电荷在不同半径处的行为、能量条件的违反情况。计算了完整光子族的阴影边界，并与M87*和Sgr A*的EHT观测数据进行对比。重点进行了赤道面顺行轨道的强偏转极限（SDL）计算，分析了临界参数、像分离、相对亮度和次领头阶时间延迟修正等观测量对正则化长度ℓ和面积半径轮廓的依赖性。

Result: 在选定的径向分支上，正的最小面积半径排除了Kerr环奇点位置，曲率不变量保持有限。两种正则几何具有相同的渐近Komar电荷但有限半径处的值不同。Einstein张量作为有效应力张量时违反零能量条件和弱能量条件。对于黑洞分支上的完整光子族，阴影边界与相同质量、自旋和观测者倾角的Kerr黑洞完全一致，两种正则几何的阴影也相同。M87*和Sgr A*的面积等效阴影直径与EHT观测区间一致，但未能进一步约束ℓ。在强偏转极限下，u_m^+和θ_∞^+对Kerr和两种正则几何是共同的，但ā_+、b̄_+、像分离、相对亮度和次领头阶时间延迟修正依赖于ℓ和面积半径轮廓。

Conclusion: 旋转Hayward型和Bardeen型正则黑洞在阴影观测上与Kerr黑洞高度简并，无法通过当前EHT数据区分。然而，在强偏转极限框架下，某些观测量（时间延迟ΔT_{2,1}、像分离s^+、相对亮度r_mag^+）对正则化长度和面积半径轮廓敏感，未来对这些量的精密测量有望检验这些旋转正则几何并将Hayward型、Bardeen型预言与Kerr区分开，包括在只存在单视界（无类Kerr内视界）的参数域中。研究表明需要超越阴影观测的更精细探测手段来约束或验证正则黑洞理论。

Abstract: We study and compare rotating Hayward-like and Bardeen-like geometries within a common Kerr-like axisymmetric ansatz, with the models distinguished by their areal-radius functions $R_i(ρ)$. On the adopted radial branches, the positive minimum areal radius excludes the Kerr ring locus, and the curvature invariants examined here remain finite. We identify the parameter domains containing two, one, or no horizons. The two geometries have the same asymptotic Komar charges but different finite-radius values. If we treat the Einstein tensor as an effective stress tensor, then for every nonzero regularity length $\ell$, this ansatz violates the null and weak energy conditions, as demonstrated by the equatorial radial-null contraction. For the complete photon family on a black-hole branch, the shadow boundary is identical to that of Kerr at the same mass, spin, and observer inclination, and is also identical for the two regular geometries. The corresponding area-equivalent shadow diameters for M87* and Sgr~A* are consistent with the approximate Event Horizon Telescope (EHT)-based intervals adopted here, and we do not obtain further constraints on $\ell$. By contrast, the prograde equatorial strong-deflection limit (SDL) calculations are only partly degenerate. At fixed spin, $u_m^+$ and $θ_\infty^+$ are common to Kerr and the two regular geometries, whereas $\bar a_+$, $\bar b_+$, the image separation, the relative magnitude, and the subleading time-delay correction depend on $\ell$ and on the areal-radius profile. Within the stated strong-deflection approximation, future measurements of $ΔT_{2,1}$, $s^+$, and $r_{\rm mag}^+$ may help test these rotating geometries and distinguish the Hayward-like and Bardeen-like predictions from Kerr, including in the one-horizon parameter domain where the adopted branch contains no Kerr-like inner horizon.

</details>


### [110] [Circular acceleration in Minkowski spacetime: thermality versus finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25277)
*Cameron R D Bunney,Jorma Louko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在2+1维闵可夫斯基时空中，空间限制如何影响圆周运动下Unruh-DeWitt探测器的响应，探讨了圆周运动Unruh效应在有边界和环境温度条件下的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unruh效应预言匀加速观察者会感受到热辐射，而圆周运动观察者也会经历类似激发。由于实验上对圆周运动Unruh效应的兴趣日益增长，特别是在模拟时空系统中进行验证的提议，需要研究实际实验条件（如空间限制边界和环境温度）如何影响探测器响应，从而为实验观测提供理论指导。

Method: 采用Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，研究在2+1维闵可夫斯基时空中作圆周运动的探测器对无质量标量场的响应。考虑标量场被限制在圆形边界内，场可处于真空态或热态。针对大边界regime建立解析结果，分析探测器响应函数，识别共振峰特征，并研究轨道速度、激发能量、边界条件和环境温度对有效温度的影响。

Result: 在大边界条件下获得了探测器响应的解析结果。发现探测器响应中存在共振峰结构，这些共振峰在场具有环境温度时更加显著。有效温度不仅依赖于加速度，还依赖于轨道速度和激发能量。空间限制边界和环境温度都会对圆周运动Unruh效应产生可观测的修正。

Conclusion: 空间限制显著改变了圆周运动中Unruh-DeWitt探测器的响应特性。在有边界和环境温度存在的实际条件下，探测器响应呈现出共振峰结构，且环境温度增强了这些共振特征。这些结果对于在模拟时空系统中实验验证圆周运动Unruh效应具有重要指导意义，表明必须考虑边界效应和环境温度的影响才能正确解释实验观测。

Abstract: The Unruh effect predicts that a uniformly linearly accelerated observer with proper acceleration $a$ reacts to the Minkowski vacuum through excitations and de-excitations with the characteristics of a thermal state at temperature $T_U=a/(2π)$. An observer in uniform circular motion will experience similar excitations and de-excitations that we may use to operationally define an effective temperature, which however depends not only on the acceleration but also on the orbital speed and excitation energy. Motivated by the experimental interest in the circular motion Unruh effect, we investigate how spatial confinement modifies the response of an Unruh-DeWitt detector in $2+1$ Minkowski spacetime. We consider a massless scalar field confined within a circular boundary prepared in either the vacuum or a thermal state, probed by an Unruh-DeWitt detector on a circular orbit, a setting that describes proposed analogue spacetime systems for testing the effect, and in which both a boundary and an ambient temperature will necessarily be present. We establish analytic results for the detector response in the large-boundary regime and identify resonance peaks, which are more prominent when the field has an ambient temperature.

</details>


### [111] [Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit: Low frequency and post-Newtonian expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25281)
*Jakob Neef,Chris Kavanagh,Adrian Ottewill*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了黑洞微扰工具包(BHPT)中Teukolsky和SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics软件包的重大更新,重点增强了弱场解析功能和后牛顿展开计算能力,为小质量比黑洞双星波形建模和黑洞微扰理论的低频展开提供了高精度计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑洞微扰工具包需要增强其解析功能,特别是在后牛顿(PN)展开方面的计算能力。为满足小质量比黑洞双星波形建模中高阶后牛顿自力计算的需求,以及黑洞微扰理论中低频展开的广泛应用需求,需要开发能够在普通笔记本电脑上实现最先进精度计算的工具。

Method: 通过更新Teukolsky软件包(v.1.2)和SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics软件包(v.1.1),实现以下功能:1)独立提取多个有用物理量(MST系数、重整化角动量、径向函数振幅、相移的低频/PM展开);2)提供齐次径向函数的PN展开,以及Kerr时空圆轨道的非齐次振幅计算;3)计算无穷远处和视界处的能量和角动量流;4)开发后牛顿自力计算工具套件;5)支持特定和通用自旋权重s以及谐波模式数ℓ和m的计算。

Result: 更新后的软件包能够在常规笔记本电脑上以最先进的精度计算各种黑洞微扰相关物理量。软件包成功提供了完整的后牛顿展开计算功能,包括齐次和非齐次解、能量角动量流、以及各种微扰理论关键参数。所有功能在BHPT的Teukolsky v.1.2和SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics v.1.1版本中可用。

Conclusion: 该软件包的增强功能为高阶后牛顿自力计算铺平了道路,这对小质量比黑洞双星波形建模具有重要意义。同时,它为黑洞微扰理论提供了广泛有用的低频展开工具,显著提升了该领域的计算能力和研究效率,使研究人员能够在普通计算设备上完成前沿的高精度计算任务。

Abstract: The Teukolsky and SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics packages of the black hole perturbation toolkit (BHPT) are one of the main public libraries to compute numerical solutions to the Teukolsky equation and related quantities. This work is a companion paper presenting their new and improved weak-field analytic functionality focusing on post-Newtonian expansions. The Teukolsky package now allows users to independently extract multiple useful quantities, such as the so called MST coefficients, renormalized angular momentum, amplitudes of the radial functions or the phase shift as a low frequency/PM expansion. It also provides PN expansions of the homogeneous radial functions or - for circular orbits in Kerr - inhomogeneous amplitudes, as well as energy and angular momentum fluxes at infinity and the horizon. All quantities can be computed to state of the art precision on regular laptops. It also provides a suite of tools for post-Newtonian self force computations and updates the analytic functionality of the SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics package. Computations are possible both for specific and generic spin weight $s$ and harmonic mode numbers $\ell$ and $m$. This package paves the way for intensive high-order post-Newtonian self-force calculations at the forefront of waveform modelling of small mass ratio black hole binaries, as well as giving widely useful low-frequency expansions for black hole perturbation theory. All functions described in this paper are available in BHPT versions Teukolsky v.1.2 and SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics v.1.1.

</details>


### [112] [Thin-Shell Black Bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25354)
*Leandro A. Lessa,Renan B. Magalhães,Gonzalo J. Olmo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Lessa和Olmo提出的黑洞反弹解在反弹处隐藏薄壳结构，并构建了一类新的具有最小面积反弹的黑洞反弹解，该反弹由薄壳支撑。研究利用量子引力中的最小长度尺度概念，采用洛伦兹型和高斯型能量密度分布，生成了一类在中心包含薄壳反弹的规则黑洞或虫洞解，并探讨了其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lessa和Olmo提出的各向异性流体黑洞反弹解在反弹处存在度规退化问题，导致逆度规不适定和曲率张量的分布不连续性。这表明在最小面积反弹处必然存在违反能量条件的薄壳物质结构，但原方案未明确揭示这一点。因此需要构建新的黑洞反弹解来正确处理反弹处的拓扑结构和物质分布。

Method: 研究采用量子引力理论中预测的基本最小长度尺度概念，使用能量密度分布来有效编码量子引力效应，将最小长度尺度上的点状物质描述替换为弥散分布。具体采用两种有物理动机的能量密度分布：洛伦兹型（Lorentzian）和高斯型（Gaussian），在静态球对称时空中构建黑洞反弹解。通过分析度规退化性、逆度规的适定性以及曲率张量的分布特性，明确薄壳在反弹处的存在及其物理性质。

Result: 成功构建了一类新的黑洞反弹解，这些解表现出规则黑洞或虫洞的特征，其中心存在由薄壳支撑的最小面积反弹。证明了反弹处必然存在违反能量条件的delta函数型薄壳物质。洛伦兹型和高斯型能量密度分布均能生成物理上自洽的解，这些解正确地描述了反弹处的非平凡拓扑结构。此外，研究还给出了这些紧致天体的热力学性质分析。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了此前黑洞反弹解中隐藏的薄壳结构，并基于量子引力的最小长度假设构建了物理上更完备的黑洞反弹解类。研究表明，在最小面积反弹处，度规退化导致必然存在违反能量条件的薄壳物质。通过采用弥散的能量密度分布（洛伦兹型和高斯型）来编码量子引力效应，可以生成同时具有规则黑洞或虫洞特征的新型紧致天体解，为理解量子引力效应如何在经典时空中体现提供了新视角。

Abstract: A recently proposed class of black-bounce solutions sourced by anisotropic fluids, whose exterior reproduces the Reissner-Nordström geometry all the way down to a minimal-area bounce, was put forward by Lessa and Olmo. In this work, we show that this solution conceals a thin-shell structure precisely at the bounce. Motivated by this observation, we construct a new class of black-bounce solutions with static, spherical symmetry featuring the emergence of a minimal-area bounce supported by a thin shell. This nontrivial topological structure is shown to arise from the degeneracy of the metric at the bounce, where the inverse metric becomes ill-defined, leading to curvature tensors with distributional discontinuities precisely at that point. As a consequence, a delta-function thin shell of energy-condition-violating matter is necessarily present at the bounce. To generate such solutions, we draw on proposals from quantum gravity that predict the existence of a fundamental minimum length scale, employing energy density profiles that encode these quantum-gravitational effects in an effective manner, replacing the point-like description of matter at minimal-length scales with a smeared one. We consider two well-motivated profiles, of Lorentzian and Gaussian type, to generate a novel class of solutions exhibiting the features of either regular black holes or wormholes, whose center conceals a thin-shell bounce. In addition, we investigate the thermodynamics of these compact objects.

</details>


### [113] [Operator-Level Quantum-Classical Correspondence in Relativistic Quantum Theory and Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25360)
*Pankaj Sheoran,Gopal Kashyap,Sanjay Siwach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子算符动力学与经典方程之间的对应关系，证明了从非相对论粒子到量子场论、线性化引力理论，Heisenberg运动方程在算符层面直接重现经典运动方程，无需取ℏ→0极限，并将此对应关系推广到弯曲时空中的场论和粒子动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已证明非相对论粒子的算符运动方程可写成与牛顿方程相同的微分形式，建立了量子算符动力学与经典力学在运动方程层面的对应关系。本文旨在探究这种对应关系是否可以推广到量子场论和线性化引力理论，以及弯曲时空背景下是否仍然成立。

Method: 采用Heisenberg绘景分析量子场算符的运动方程，系统地研究广泛类别场论（包括Klein-Gordon场、电磁场、Yang-Mills场）的算符演化方程；在弱场近似下考察度规扰动的Heisenberg演化；将分析扩展到弯曲时空，研究量子场算符是否满足相应的协变场方程；在半经典极限下考察弯曲时空中量子粒子的位置和动量算符的Heisenberg方程。

Result: 明确证明了对于广泛类别的场论，量子场算符的Heisenberg运动方程在算符层面直接重现了相应的经典场方程（Klein-Gordon方程、Maxwell方程组、Yang-Mills方程、线性化Einstein方程），无需取ℏ→0极限。在弯曲时空中，量子场算符在算符层面满足相应的协变场方程。在弱场近似下，度规扰动的Heisenberg演化在算符层面重现线性化Einstein方程。对于弯曲时空中的量子粒子，其位置和动量的Heisenberg方程在半经典极限下重现经典测地线运动。

Conclusion: 本文成功将算符层面的量子-经典对应关系从非相对论粒子推广到量子场论、线性化引力以及弯曲时空背景。这种对应关系表明，经典场方程（包括电磁场、Yang-Mills场和线性化引力场方程）可以直接从量子场算符的Heisenberg运动方程中得出，无需经典极限，揭示了量子理论与经典理论之间更深层次的联系，为理解量子场论的经典极限和量子引力的半经典行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: In a recent work \cite{Shaikh:2026nsi}, it was shown that for a non-relativistic particle, the operator equations of motion can be cast in the same differential form as Newton's equation of motion. This establishes a correspondence between quantum operator dynamics and classical mechanics at the level of the equations of motion. In this work, we investigate whether this correspondence extends to quantum field theory and linearized gravity. We explicitly show that the Heisenberg equations of motion for quantum field operators reproduce the corresponding classical equations of motion for a broad class of field theories. The Klein-Gordon equation, Maxwell's equations, Yang-Mills equations, and linearized Einstein equations are shown to arise at the operator level without taking the limit $\hbar\rightarrow0$. We then extend the analysis to curved spacetime and show that the quantum field operators satisfy the corresponding covariant field equations at the operator level. In the weak-field approximation, we show that the Heisenberg evolution of the metric-perturbations reproduces the linearized Einstein equations at the operator level. For a quantum particle in curved spacetime, the Heisenberg equations for position and momentum reproduce classical geodesic motion in the semiclassical limit, thereby extending the operator-level quantum-classical correspondence from fields to particle dynamics.

</details>


### [114] [Cosmology in sixth-order higher-derivative theories of gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25387)
*Carlos Aguilar,Manuel Asorey,Diego Muñoz,Miguel Pardina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析了包含二次曲率不变量和六阶导数算符的引力理论的宇宙学意义,研究了其宇宙学解、稳定性以及对暴胀观测量的修正,发现六阶算符在保持有效场论展开有效性的同时,对Starobinsky模型产生可控偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高阶导数引力理论在宇宙学中的应用,特别是研究包含R²、Weyl张量平方以及六阶导数算符(R□R和C^{μνρσ}□C_{μνρσ})的引力理论如何影响宇宙演化、奇点规避机制以及暴胀观测量,从而为通过宇宙学观测探测紫外引力修正提供理论框架。

Method: 1) 在Einstein框架下分析均匀各向同性宇宙学解,研究膨胀、再坍缩和无奇点反弹解;2) 分析标准背景上张量扰动的线性方程,通过传播子的极点结构研究稳定性;3) 研究标量宇宙学扰动在de Sitter时空中的超视界不稳定性;4) 采用有效场论(EFT)方法,将六阶项视为Starobinsky背景的微扰修正,计算原初标量和张量功率谱的修正,提取标量谱指数和张标比等暴胀观测量。

Result: 理论允许膨胀、再坍缩和无奇点反弹解;高阶导数自由度在规避Hawking-Penrose奇点定理中起关键作用;张量扰动的完整线性方程组存在解析稳定解,稳定性由传播子极点结构和高阶导数耦合常数间的特定关系决定,但无奇点背景对张量扰动不稳定;二次和六阶引力在de Sitter时空中均呈现标量超视界不稳定性;六阶算符对Starobinsky模型的标量谱指数和张标比产生可控偏离,同时保持EFT展开的一致性。

Conclusion: 包含二次和六阶曲率算符的高阶导数引力理论提供了丰富的宇宙学现象学,虽然无奇点背景存在不稳定性问题,但通过EFT方法将六阶项作为微扰处理,可以在Starobinsky模型基础上获得对暴胀观测量的可控修正,为通过宇宙学观测探测紫外引力效应提供了一致的唯象学框架。

Abstract: We analyze the cosmological implications of theories of gravity with quadratic curvature invariants $R^2$ and $C^{μνρσ}C_{μνρσ}$, and six-derivative operators $R\Box R$ and $C^{μνρσ}\Box C_{μνρσ}$. We first analyze homogeneous and isotropic cosmological solutions, showing that the theory admits expanding, recollapsing and non-singular bouncing solutions. We clarify in the Einstein-frame formulation the mechanism that circumvents the Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems, and identify the role played by the additional higher-derivative degrees of freedom in that mechanism. We show that the complete set of linear equations governing tensor perturbations on standard backgrounds admit analytically stable solutions. The analysis reveals that the stability properties are determined by the pole structure of the propagator and by specific relations among the higher-derivative couplings, while the singularity-free backgrounds are found to be unstable against tensor perturbations. For scalar cosmological perturbations, the higher-derivative theories presents super-horizon scalar instabilities in de Sitter spacetimes for both quadratic and sixth-order gravity. To extract inflationary observables, we adopt an EFT approach to the sixth-order theory, treating these terms as perturbative corrections around the Starobinsky background. Within this framework, we compute the modification of the primordial scalar and tensor power spectra, obtaining predictions for the scalar spectral index and tensor-to-scalar ratio. We find that sixth-order operators produce controlled deviations from the Starobinsky model while preserving the EFT expansion, thereby providing a consistent phenomenological framework to probe ultraviolet gravitational corrections through cosmological observables.

</details>


### [115] [A Systematic Study of Resonance-Driven Flux Modifications in Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25392)
*Edoardo Levati,Lennox S. Keeble,Alejandro Cárdenas-Avendaño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋近系统(EMRIs)中瞬态轨道共振对引力波信号的影响,通过求解Teukolsky方程计算了大范围参数空间内的共振系数,为未来EMRIs共振研究提供了迄今最大的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬态轨道共振会对极端质量比旋近系统的长期动力学演化和引力波信号产生相位依赖的修正。为了准确预测EMRIs的引力波波形,需要量化共振引起的能量、轴向角动量和Carter常数通量的修正,并系统性地计算广泛参数空间内的共振系数。

Method: 使用公开的pybhpt代码在频域求解Teukolsky方程,相干组合共振时出现的简并径向和极向谐波。解析推导了控制共振通量修正的径向-极向相对相位依赖性的选择定则。针对动力学上重要的3:2和2:1共振,系统研究了共振系数随主黑洞自旋、轨道偏心率和倾角的变化规律。

Result: 成功计算了广泛轨道参数空间内能量、轴向角动量和Carter常数通量的共振修正系数,获得了迄今为止基于Teukolsky方程计算的最大共振系数数据集。发现不同共振的相对强度反映了对称性导致的抵消效应与共振轨道对二维轨道相空间采样程度之间的平衡。表征了3:2和2:1共振系数与黑洞自旋、偏心率和倾角的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了EMRIs系统中瞬态轨道共振效应的系统性量化结果,建立了基于Teukolsky方程的最完整共振系数数据库。研究揭示了共振强度的物理机制,即对称性抵消与相空间采样之间的竞争关系。这些结果为未来EMRIs中瞬态轨道共振的研究提供了重要的理论输入,对准确预测引力波探测器可观测的EMRI波形具有关键意义。

Abstract: Transient orbital resonances can introduce phase-dependent corrections to the evolution of extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), potentially altering their long-term dynamics and emitted gravitational-wave signals. In this work, we quantify the resonance-induced modifications to the energy, axial angular momentum, and Carter constant fluxes and compute the corresponding resonance coefficients across a broad region of the orbital parameter space. Using the publicly available \texttt{pybhpt} code, we solve the Teukolsky equation in the frequency domain to coherently combine the degenerate radial and polar harmonics that arise at resonance. We analytically derive a selection rule governing the relative radial-polar phase dependence of the resonant flux modifications. We argue that the relative strengths of different resonances reflect a balance between symmetry-induced cancellations and the degree to which the resonant orbit samples the underlying two-dimensional orbital phase space. For the dynamically important $3{:}2$ and $2{:}1$ resonances, we also characterize how the resonance coefficients vary with the primary black-hole spin, orbital eccentricity and inclination. Our results constitute the largest set of Teukolsky-based resonance coefficients calculated to date and provide essential input for future studies of transient orbital resonances in EMRIs.

</details>


### [116] [Analytical and spectral characterization of floral patterns in higher-order polynomial pp-waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25459)
*F. L. Carneiro,H. P. de Carvalho,L. A. Cabral,M. P. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高阶多项式模式pp波引起的粒子环花瓣状形变，通过解析和数值方法分析了横向潮汐本征方向、弱脉冲响应、测地线积分和傅里叶分解，揭示了形变由基本角模式主导且具有离散对称性的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力波高阶多项式模式（pp波）对粒子环结构产生的形变效应，特别是理解不同角模式m如何决定花瓣状形变模式的几何特征和对称性，以及区分位移记忆和速度记忆效应的时间特征。

Method: 采用解析推导和数值计算相结合的方法：(1)利用横向潮汐本征方向分析确定径向拉伸和压缩扇区；(2)构建弱脉冲解获得粒子环的一阶完整响应；(3)对m=2,3,4模式进行直接测地线积分验证预测的方向和离散对称性；(4)应用傅里叶分析分解响应的角模式成分；(5)通过脉冲时间矩分析区分位移记忆和速度记忆效应。

Result: 研究发现：(1)横向潮汐本征方向决定了m个交错的径向拉伸和压缩扇区；(2)m=2,3,4的测地线积分结果证实了预测的方向和离散对称性；(3)傅里叶分析表明响应绝对性地由角模式n=m主导；(4)m=4时出现的环状结构源于基本模式的几何折叠而非强谐波混合；(5)脉冲的时间矩可以区分位移记忆和速度记忆，但不改变角向特征。

Conclusion: 高阶多项式模式pp波引起的粒子环形变呈现明确的花瓣状模式，其角向结构由基本角模式n=m绝对主导，具有预测的离散对称性。复杂的环状几何特征（如m=4的情况）本质上源于基本模式的几何折叠效应而非高阶谐波的强耦合。时间矩分析能够有效区分不同类型的引力波记忆效应，而不影响其固有的角向对称性特征。

Abstract: We investigate analytically and numerically the floral deformation of particle rings induced by higher-order polynomial modes of pp-waves. The transverse tidal eigendirections determine $m$ interlaced sectors of radial stretching and compression, while a weak-pulse solution provides the complete first-order response of the ring. Direct geodesic integration for $m=2,3,4$ confirms the predicted orientation and discrete symmetry. A Fourier analysis shows that the response remains overwhelmingly dominated by the angular mode $n=m$; in particular, the loops found for $m=4$ arise from geometrical folding of the fundamental mode rather than strong harmonic mixing. The temporal moments of the pulse further distinguish displacement from velocity memory without changing this angular signature.

</details>


### [117] [Light Cone Thermodynamics: Quasi-Local Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25477)
*Matheus G. Barbosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Bondi-Sachs形式推广到任意世界线相关的过去光锥,建立了广义时空中的准局域热力学形式体系,证明了准局域情境下的霍金辐射发射,论证了不可逆不等式的满足,并构建了包含时空动力学的广义经典热力学公设框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受黑洞热力学和其他表明广义相对论与热力学存在内在联系的研究结果启发,本文旨在建立适用于一般时空的准局域热力学形式体系,以探索引力系统的热力学性质及其与时空动力学的深层联系。

Method: 将Bondi-Sachs形式推广到与任意世界线相关的过去光锉;通过多个实例演示准局域情境下的霍金辐射发射;论证典型引力系统中"不可逆不等式"的满足;基于Landau-Lifshitz赝张量导出内能定义;构建广义经典热力学的公设框架以纳入时空动力学。

Result: 成功在准局域情境下证明了霍金辐射可能在温和条件下发生;确立了引力系统中类似热力学第二定律的不可逆不等式;建立了包含时空动力学的广义热力学框架;实现了从热力学变量定义静质量密度,使其与组分数动力学解耦。

Conclusion: 本研究通过将Bondi-Sachs形式推广至一般时空情境,成功构建了准局域热力学形式体系,揭示了引力系统的热力学行为(包括霍金辐射和类第二定律),并建立了统一描述时空动力学与热力学的理论框架,为理解广义相对论与热力学的内在联系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Inspired by black hole thermodynamics and other results suggesting that general relativity has some intrinsic connection with thermodynamics, this work aims to establish a quasi-local thermodynamic formalism in general spacetimes. With this purpose, we generalize the Bondi-Sachs formalism to past light cones associated with an arbitrary worldline. From this generalization, we demonstrate the emission of Hawking radiation in a quasi-local context through a few examples, suggesting that it may occur under mild conditions. In addition, we argue that an "irreversible inequality" is satisfied in typical gravitational systems, indicating that an analogue of the second law may be established. Together with a definition of internal energy derived from the Landau-Lifshitz pseudotensor, these results are used to construct a postulational framework that generalizes classical thermodynamics in order to include the dynamics of spacetime. As a consequence, it is possible to define the rest-mass density from thermodynamic variables, decoupling it from the dynamics of constituent numbers.

</details>


### [118] [Quadratic Gravitational-Wave Scattering by Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25522)
*Lennox S. Keeble,Hengrui Zhu,Lawrence E. Kidder,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Mark A. Scheel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用数值相对论研究Kerr黑洞对近单色入射引力波的二阶非线性响应，测量了不同自旋下散射态的二次自耦合，并通过半解析二阶Teukolsky计算验证结果，为精密引力波建模和强场引力检验提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波探测器需要精密的引力波模型和强场引力检验，这依赖于二阶黑洞扰动理论。现有研究主要集中在准正则模式(QNMs)的二次相互作用上，而对Kerr黑洞中实频散射态的非线性相互作用研究相对较少。需要系统研究散射态的二次耦合特性，以建立完整的二阶扰动理论框架。

Method: 采用数值相对论方法研究Kerr黑洞对近单色(ℓ,m)=(2,±2)入射引力波的二次响应，测量自旋a高达0.95时向外传播的(ℓ,m)=(4,4)子模在两倍母频率处的复自耦合系数。通过半解析的二阶Teukolsky方程计算补充和验证数值相对论模拟结果，系统分析不同频率和自旋参数下的非线性耦合特性。

Result: 在低频时，耦合被强烈抑制且顺行和逆行散射表现出相反的自旋依赖性。在较高顺行频率处发现子模共振，其频率和宽度与基本(ℓ,m,n)=(4,4,0) QNM一致。在基本(2,2,0) QNM频率附近，入射模耦合随自旋增长，与二次QNM耦合的递减趋势相反，表明非线性响应依赖于完整的母散射态而非仅频率。半解析计算成功重现数值相对论的非线性响应结果。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了Kerr黑洞二阶齐次辐射扰动的数值和半解析理论框架，揭示了散射态非线性耦合的复杂自旋和频率依赖关系，证明非线性响应由完整散射态决定而非仅由频率决定。研究成果可应用于动力学潮汐、近极端动力学和二阶引力自力理论，为下一代引力波探测器的精密建模和强场引力检验提供重要理论支撑。

Abstract: Second-order black-hole perturbation theory will be an important component of precision gravitational-wave modeling and tests of strong-field gravity with next-generation detectors. Beyond quasinormal modes (QNMs), whose quadratic interactions have been the focus of recent work in black-hole spectroscopy, a generic retarded solution consists of a continuum of real-frequency scattering states whose nonlinear interactions in Kerr remain comparatively less explored. We use numerical relativity to study the quadratic response of Kerr black holes to nearly monochromatic $(\ell,m)=(2,\pm2)$ incident gravitational waves, measuring the complex self-coupling to the outgoing $(\ell,m)=(4,4)$ daughter at twice the parent frequency for black-hole spins up to $a=0.95$. At low frequencies, the coupling is strongly suppressed and exhibits opposite spin dependence for prograde and retrograde scattering. At higher prograde frequencies, we identify a daughter-mode resonance whose frequency and width extracted from the nonlinear response track the fundamental $(\ell,m,n)=(4,4,0)$ QNM. Near the fundamental $(2,2,0)$ QNM frequency, the in-mode coupling grows with spin, in contrast to the decreasing quadratic QNM coupling, demonstrating that the nonlinear response depends on the full parent scattering state rather than on its frequency alone. A complementary semi-analytic second-order Teukolsky calculation reproduces the nonlinear response from our numerical relativity simulations. Our numerical-relativity results and their semi-analytic extension provide a basis for generic homogeneous radiative perturbations of Kerr at second order, with applications to dynamical tides, near-extremal dynamics, and second-order gravitational self-force theory.

</details>


### [119] [Axial Perturbations of Charged Kiselev Black Holes in Rastall Gravity: Matter Response and Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25565)
*Yan Wang,Yun-tao Gu,Wen-Di Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rastall引力中被尘埃类Kiselev型各向异性物质包围的带电黑洞的轴向引力电磁准正规模式，通过Chebyshev伪谱方法计算发现Rastall耦合参数和周围物质对准正规模频率和阻尼率具有非平凡的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论（Rastall引力）框架下，研究被各向异性物质包围的带电黑洞的准正规模式，以理解修正引力参数、黑洞电荷和周围物质对黑洞振荡特性的综合影响，这对检验广义相对论的替代理论和理解黑洞在复杂物质环境中的动力学行为具有重要意义。

Method: 采用闭合处理忽略各向异性方向的扰动，将系统约化为两个耦合的薛定谔型方程；引入辅助的冻结源截断系统作为对比参考；针对轴向偶极模式分析物质响应系统的单向耦合特性；使用Chebyshev伪谱方法进行数值计算，并通过Reissner-Nordström极限和谱收敛性测试验证计算的有效性。

Result: 在小γ区域，增大无量纲Rastall耦合参数γ会同时降低振荡频率和阻尼率；在大γ区域，振荡频率上升，而阻尼率呈现非单调变化。较大的黑洞电荷通常会提高振荡频率，而更强的周围物质贡献倾向于产生更长寿命的模式。物质响应系统与参考截断系统的基频差异仅在亚百分比水平。所有研究的基本轴向模式均满足Im(ω)<0，表明稳定性。

Conclusion: Rastall修正和周围各向异性物质的存在通常会阻碍方程的代数解耦，但通过数值方法可以有效求解耦合系统。Rastall耦合参数对准正规模频率和阻尼的影响呈现区域依赖的复杂行为，黑洞电荷和周围物质也以特定方式调制振荡特性。所有轴向模式的稳定性得到确认，为在修正引力和复杂物质环境中理解带电黑洞的动力学提供了新的理论洞察。

Abstract: We investigate axial gravitoelectromagnetic quasinormal modes of a charged black hole surrounded by dust-like Kiselev-type anisotropic matter in Rastall gravity. Under a closure prescription that neglects perturbations of the anisotropy direction, the system reduces to two coupled Schrödinger-type equations. These equations admit an $r$-independent algebraic decoupling in the Reissner--Nordström and general-relativistic dust-like limits, but such a decoupling is generically obstructed when both the Rastall modification and the surrounding-matter contribution are present. For comparison, we introduce an auxiliary frozen-source truncation, whose potential matrix differs from the matter-response matrix in only two entries. For the axial dipole, the matter-response system has one-way coupling, whereas the truncated reference system has two-way coupling. Nevertheless, representative fundamental frequencies of the matter-response system and the reference system differ only at the sub-percent level. A Chebyshev pseudospectral calculation, validated through the Reissner--Nordström limit and spectral-convergence tests, shows that increasing the dimensionless Rastall coupling $γ$ lowers both the oscillation frequency and the damping rate in the small-$γ$ region. In the large-$γ$ region, the oscillation frequency rises, whereas the damping rate varies nonmonotonically. A larger black-hole charge generally raises the oscillation frequency, whereas stronger surrounding-matter contributions tend to produce longer-lived modes. All surveyed fundamental axial modes satisfy $\operatorname{Im}ω<0$.

</details>


### [120] [Lensing Signatures of a Primary Hair Black Hole Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25667)
*Rahul Das,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了引力退耦方法框架下初级毛发黑洞的弱场和强场引力透镜效应,通过对Schwarzschild时空施加几何形变构造黑洞解,并利用Sgr A*的观测数据约束了初级毛发参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力退耦方法产生的初级毛发黑洞的引力透镜性质,研究初级毛发参数Q和耦合常数α如何改变Schwarzschild时空的透镜效应,并利用事件视界望远镜对Sgr A*的观测数据对理论参数进行约束。

Method: 采用引力退耦方法对Schwarzschild几何施加几何形变构造黑洞解,通过主导能量条件确定形变函数。在弱场区域使用Gibbons-Werner方法计算偏折角的二阶修正;在强场区域采用Bozza形式计算光子球附近的偏折角及相对论性成像。以超大质量黑洞Sgr A*为透镜,分析强引力透镜观测量(渐近角位置、角间距、相对星等),并利用EHT合作组的Sgr A*阴影观测数据约束参数。

Result: 弱场情况下,耦合参数α对主导阶偏折角的贡献显著大于初级毛发参数Q,且初级毛发表现出有效排斥性引力行为。强场情况下,偏折角在光子球附近呈对数发散,光子可绕黑洞多圈形成相对论性像。通过Sgr A*的强透镜观测量和EHT阴影观测,成功对不同耦合常数α值下的初级毛发参数Q进行了约束。

Conclusion: 引力退耦方法产生的初级毛发黑洞在引力透镜效应中表现出与Schwarzschild黑洞的显著差异,耦合参数α在弱场中起主导作用,初级毛发具有排斥性质。强场区域的相对论性成像特征和EHT对Sgr A*的观测为约束该理论的特征参数提供了有效途径,验证了该黑洞模型的天体物理学可行性。

Abstract: We investigate the weak and strong field gravitational lensing of light by a primary hair black hole (BH) solution obtained within the framework of the gravitational decoupling (GD) approach. In this method, the BH solution is constructed by introducing geometric deformations to a known seed spacetime, which, in the present case, is the Schwarzschild geometry. The deformation functions are determined by imposing the dominant energy condition, leading to the emergence of two characteristic parameters, the primary hair $Q$ and the coupling constant $α$, both of which deform the Schwarzschild spacetime. To explore the lensing properties of this BH, we employ the Gibbons-Werner approach in the weak field regime and Bozza's formalism in the strong field regime. In the weak field limit, we derive the deflection angle up to second-order corrections and find that the coupling parameter $α$ contributes more significantly to the leading-order deflection than the primary hair parameter $Q$. The primary hair is also found to exhibit an effectively repulsive gravitational behavior. In the strong field regime, we compute the deflection angle of photons propagating near the photon sphere, where the deflection diverges logarithmically, allowing photons to complete multiple loops around the BH and form relativistic images. Using the supermassive BH Sgr A* as the astrophysical lens, we further investigate the strong lensing observables, namely the asymptotic angular position $\vartheta_{\infty}$, the angular separation $s$, and the relative magnitude $r_{\mathrm{mag}}$. We also constrain the primary hair parameter $Q$ for different values of the coupling constant $α$ using the shadow observations of Sgr A* reported by the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) Collaboration.

</details>


### [121] [Thermodynamics of arbitrary spherical surfaces in FLRW spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25763)
*Xun Gong,Jun Wang,Chen-Yang Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论中非平坦FLRW时空内任意球对称表面的热力学，证明了引力-热力学对应关系是引力动力学的普遍特征，而非因果视界独有的性质，并解决了宇宙学事件视界热力学中的先前不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于宇宙学事件视界热力学存在不一致性和争议，需要明确引力-热力学对应关系是否仅限于因果视界，还是适用于更普遍的球对称表面。现有研究对事件视界温度的处理存在现象学修正和理论缺陷的质疑，需要从引力场方程本身出发给出自洽的热力学描述。

Method: 采用"引力到热力学"的研究视角，直接从引力场方程和物质动力学推导克劳修斯关系和统一第一定律。关键方法包括：利用能量-动量守恒导出物质流量度量；分析非平坦FLRW时空中任意球对称表面的热力学性质；系统考察宇宙学表观视界和事件视界热力学作为特殊情况；从引力动力学推导事件视界温度，并验证其在de Sitter极限下退化为Gibbons-Hawking温度。

Result: 成功证明克劳修斯关系和统一第一定律可直接从引力场方程导出，无需引入现象学修正。从引力动力学推导的事件视界温度同时满足两个热力学关系，并在de Sitter极限下正确退化为Gibbons-Hawking温度。揭示了先前不一致性源于不兼容的温度定义方式，而非事件视界热力学本身存在内在缺陷。宇宙学表观视界和事件视界热力学均作为一般理论的特例被恢复。

Conclusion: 引力-热力学对应关系是引力动力学的普遍特征，适用于非平坦FLRW时空中的任意球对称表面，而不仅限于因果视界。宇宙学事件视界热力学在采用正确的温度定义后是自洽的，先前的不一致问题来自温度处方的不兼容而非理论本身的缺陷。该研究为引力热力学提供了更加统一和普遍的理论框架。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the thermodynamics of arbitrary spherically symmetric surfaces in non-flat FLRW spacetimes within general relativity. Adopting the gravity-to-thermodynamics perspective, the Clausius relation and the unified first law follow directly from the gravitational field equations and matter dynamics, with a central role played by the matter-flux measure derived from energy-momentum conservation. The cosmological apparent-horizon and event-horizon thermodynamics are both recovered as special cases. In particular, the event-horizon temperatures derived from the gravitational dynamics satisfy both thermodynamic relations without introducing phenomenological modifications, and the corresponding temperature reduces to the Gibbons-Hawking temperature in the de Sitter limit. We further show that previous inconsistencies originate from incompatible temperature prescriptions rather than from any intrinsic deficiency in the thermodynamics of the cosmological event horizon. These results demonstrate that the gravity-thermodynamics correspondence is a generic feature of gravitational dynamics rather than a property unique to causal horizons.

</details>


### [122] [Radiation-pressure induced chaos in multipendular Fabry-Perot resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25872)
*V. Pierro,I. M. Pinto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了多摆式Fabry-Perot谐振腔(无光传播延迟)中的混沌现象，估计了从稳定平衡点附近初始条件演化出混沌解的概率，并探讨了其与系统关键参数的依赖关系及在干涉引力波天线中的潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多摆式Fabry-Perot谐振腔系统中混沌行为的发生机制，以及从稳定平衡点演化为混沌态的概率特征，这对理解干涉引力波天线等精密光学系统的动力学稳定性具有重要意义。

Method: 通过数值模拟和动力学分析方法，研究无光传播延迟的多摆式FP谐振腔系统的混沌演化行为；从稳定平衡点附近的初始条件出发，估算在给定时间内演化为混沌解的概率；系统性地分析该概率与系统最相关参数之间的依赖关系。

Result: 成功展示了多摆式FP谐振腔中混沌现象的发生；定量估计了从稳定平衡点附近初始条件演化为混沌态的概率；揭示了该概率与系统关键参数之间的依赖规律。

Conclusion: 多摆式Fabry-Perot谐振腔系统存在明显的混沌行为，从稳定平衡点演化为混沌态的概率与系统参数密切相关。这些研究结果对干涉引力波天线的设计和稳定性控制具有重要的参考价值，有助于理解和预防精密光学系统中可能出现的混沌不稳定性问题。

Abstract: The occurrence of chaos in multipendular FP resonators (without light propagation delay) is illustrated. The probability of evolving a chaotic solution in a given time, starting from initial conditions contained in a neighbourhood of a stable equilibrium point, is estimated, and its dependence on the most relevant system parameters is investigated. The possible relevance of these results for interferometric gravitational wave antennas is discussed.

</details>


### [123] [Spacetimes of maximal proper acceleration and muon mean lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25903)
*Ricardo Gallego Torromé*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在最大固有加速度时空中μ子平均寿命的缩短现象,发现当最大固有加速度等同于施温格加速度时,该效应可在未来的μ子对撞机设施中被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索最大固有加速度时空对基本粒子衰变性质的影响,特别是研究μ子寿命在极端加速条件下的行为,这对理解量子场论在强加速场中的表现以及验证相关理论预言具有重要意义。

Method: 理论分析方法:考察具有最大固有加速度的时空模型,针对多个最大固有加速度候选值进行计算,重点分析当最大固有加速度与施温格加速度相等时的情况,推导加速度对μ子平均寿命的修正效应。

Result: 对于所有考虑的最大固有加速度候选值,诱导的修正效应都较小。然而,当最大固有加速度被识别为施温格加速度时,μ子平均寿命的缩短效应在数值上达到了可观测的量级,能够在未来的μ子对撞机设施中被探测到。

Conclusion: 在最大固有加速度时空框架下,μ子的平均寿命会发生缩短。尽管大多数情况下修正较小,但当最大固有加速度等同于施温格加速度(约10^18 m/s²)时,该效应具有实验可探测性,为未来μ子对撞机实验提供了一个可验证的理论预言,有助于检验极端加速条件下的时空结构和量子场论效应。

Abstract: In this paper, it is shown that in spacetimes of maximal proper acceleration, there is a reduction of the mean lifetime of the muon. We consider several candidates for the maximal proper acceleration. For all of them, the induced corrections are small, but for the case when the maximal proper acceleration is identified with the Schwinger's acceleration, the effect is detectable in future muon collider facilities.

</details>


### [124] [Gravitational Imprints of Dark Energy in Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25910)
*O. P. Jyothilakshmi,Lakshmi J. Naik,V. Sreekanth*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次采用完全广义相对论方法研究了具有暗能量核心的中子星非径向f模振荡，发现暗能量的加入软化了状态方程并降低了最大质量，f模频率和潮汐形变性与引力波观测数据一致，预测的引力波信号位于第三代探测器的灵敏度范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内部可能存在奇异物质或暗能量成分，但此前尚未有研究在完全广义相对论框架下系统探讨暗能量核心对中子星非径向f模振荡的影响。理解暗能量核心如何改变中子星的振荡特性、潮汐形变性及引力波特征，对于解释观测数据（如GW170817、GW190814等引力波事件和脉冲星观测）以及探测暗能量在致密天体中的作用具有重要意义。

Method: 采用相对论平均场模型描述核物质，使用修正的Chaplygin流体处方描述暗能量成分，构建含暗能量核心的中子星模型。通过改变暗能量状态方程参数和转变密度，计算恒星结构、f模振荡频率和阻尼时间、潮汐形变性，以及引力波能量和应变振幅。采用完全广义相对论处理方法分析非径向f模振荡特性。

Result: 暗能量的加入软化了状态方程，降低了中子星的最大质量，质量-半径关系与PSR J0030+0451和PSR J0740+6620等大质量脉冲星观测一致。暗能量核心的范围（由转变密度决定）对恒星结构和振荡性质有关键影响，较低转变密度在更宽质量范围内产生显著变化。对于较大质量构型，f模频率和阻尼时间表现出系统性改变，f模频率与潮汐形变性的关联与GW170817和GW190814观测约束一致。归一化振荡能量分布保持近似普适性，仅在高质量构型下出现微小偏离。预测的f模振荡特征引力波应变位于未来第三代引力波探测器的灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在完全广义相对论框架下证明，含暗能量核心的中子星模型能够同时满足脉冲星质量-半径观测和引力波事件的约束条件。暗能量核心通过软化状态方程系统性地改变了中子星的振荡特性和潮汐形变性，但保持了f模频率-潮汐形变性关联的普适性。转变密度是控制暗能量影响程度的关键参数。研究预测的引力波信号特征为未来第三代探测器（如Einstein Telescope和Cosmic Explorer）探测中子星内部暗能量成分提供了可观测窗口。

Abstract: We investigate, for the first time, the non-radial $f$-mode oscillations of neutron stars (NSs) with a dark energy (DE) core utilising a fully general relativistic treatment. We construct the stellar profile by considering a relativistic mean field model for nuclear matter and a modified Chaplygin fluid like prescription for DE. We compute the stellar structure, $f$-mode oscillations, tidal deformability, and gravitational wave (GW) energy and strain of the NS with DE core by varying the DE equation of state (EoS) parameters. Our study reveals that the inclusion of DE softens EoS and reduces the maximum mass compared to the pure neutron star. The obtained mass-radius values are consistent with observational constraints from massive pulsars such as PSR J0030+0451 and PSR J0740+6620. We find that the extent of the DE core, determined by the transition density, plays a crucial role in modifying the stellar structure and oscillation properties, with lower transition densities producing appreciable changes over a wider mass range. The $f$-mode frequencies and damping times exhibit systematic modifications, for higher mass configurations, while the correlation between the $f$-mode frequency and tidal deformability remains consistent with observational constraints from GW170817 and GW190814. Further, the normalised oscillation energy distribution remains nearly universal, with only minimal deviations for the higher mass configurations. Finally, we estimate the characteristic GW strain associated with the $f$-mode oscillations and find that the predicted signals lie within the sensitivity band of future third generation GW detectors.

</details>


### [125] [Weakly nonlinear strong-field signatures of spontaneously vectorized Einstein-vector-Gauss-Bonnet black holes near the Schwarzschild bifurcation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26266)
*Sardor Murodov,Bekzod Rahmatov,Islom Egamberdiev,Dilnavoz Kamalova,Javlon Rayimbaev,Bobomurat Ahmedov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了接近Schwarzschild分岔点的自发矢量化Einstein-矢量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞的静态球对称电荷分支，通过弱非线性展开方法计算了临界耦合、度规修正和ADM质量修正，并分析了光子球、阴影、轨道等关键物理量的修正，揭示了电荷矢量化分支的局部强场几何特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Einstein-矢量-Gauss-Bonnet理论中的自发矢量化黑洞提供了超越Schwarzschild解的新型黑洞解，在分岔点附近研究其电荷分支的性质对于理解该理论的强场行为至关重要。通过系统性地分析矢量化分支偏离分岔点的行为，可以刻画这类修正引力理论黑洞的局部强场几何结构，为理解矢量场自发破缺对黑洞时空的影响提供理论基础。

Method: 采用弱非线性展开方法围绕基本矢量零模进行分析。使用打靶法(shooting)和配置法(collocation)独立确定临界耦合常数。通过协变方程检验二阶度规修正的正确性，解析证明相应的ADM质量修正为零。利用三阶Fredholm可解性条件确定矢量化分支偏离分岔点的主导行为。基于得到的度规，计算光子球、阴影尺度、边界束缚轨道、最内稳定圆轨道、轨道频率和本轮频率、三比二频率比半径、圆光子轨道不稳定性以及对数强偏转系数等物理量的主导修正。

Result: 在分岔点附近，光子球和最内稳定轨道向外移动，而三比二频率比半径向内移动。光子轨道角频率降低，其Lyapunov指数增加。二阶ADM质量修正解析地为零。通过两种独立方法(打靶法和配置法)成功确定了临界耦合常数，并且二阶度规修正通过了完整协变方程的验证。这些结果系统地刻画了电荷矢量化分支的局部强场几何特性。

Conclusion: 研究成功建立了自发矢量化Einstein-矢量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞电荷分支在Schwarzschild分岔点附近的系统性理论描述。通过弱非线性展开和多种数值方法的交叉验证，获得了可靠的度规修正和一系列关键物理量的修正结果。这些结果揭示了矢量场自发破缺如何改变黑洞附近的强场几何结构，为理解修正引力理论中的黑洞物理提供了重要的理论基础。需要强调的是，这些结果描述的是分岔点附近的局部行为,不应外推至强非线性区域或直接用作观测约束。

Abstract: We study the static, spherically symmetric electric branch of spontaneously vectorized Einstein-vector-Gauss-Bonnet black holes close to its Schwarzschild bifurcation. A weakly nonlinear expansion is built around the fundamental vector zero mode. The critical coupling is determined independently by shooting and collocation, the second-order metric correction is checked against the unreduced covariant equations, and the corresponding ADM-mass correction is shown to vanish analytically. The leading departure of the vectorized branch from the bifurcation point follows from a third-order Fredholm solvability condition. Using the resulting metric, we calculate the leading corrections to the photon sphere, shadow scale, marginally bound orbit, innermost stable circular orbit, orbital and epicyclic frequencies, the geometric three-to-two frequency-ratio radius, the instability of the circular photon orbit, and the logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient. Near the bifurcation, the photon sphere and innermost stable orbit move outward, whereas the three-to-two radius moves inward. The photon-orbit angular frequency decreases and its Lyapunov exponent increases. These results characterize the local strong-field geometry of the electric vectorized branch. They are not intended as observational constraints or as an extrapolation to the strongly nonlinear regime.

</details>


### [126] [Area-product universality in multi-horizon black hole entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26289)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Y. Gómez-Leyton,Jean Báez Cuevas,Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了黑洞熵与多视界面积乘积关系之间的深层联系：通过将统计离散化方法扩展到多Killing视界，发现对数修正项探测视界面积的乘积，而领头的Bekenstein-Hawking熵探测面积之和，从而建立了微观熵统计与经典几何普适性之间的结构性关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞熵和多视界面积乘积关系分别源于微观统计和经典几何，看似是两种独立的普适性表现。然而这两种结构之间是否存在更深层的联系尚不清楚。研究者希望探索微观熵计数与经典多视界几何之间可能存在的内在关联，特别是在领头面积定律之外，对数修正项是否编码了关于全局视界结构的独特信息。

Method: 采用最小假设统计离散化方法扩展到多Killing视界系统，将不同视界扇区视为统计独立的部分。通过分析领头Bekenstein-Hawking熵和对数修正项对视界面积的依赖关系，推导出一般的面积乘积规则。针对Reissner-Nordström黑洞、Kerr-Newman黑洞(通过基于面积的扩展处理旋转视界)以及Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞等具体案例进行验证和分析,检验经典面积乘积的质量独立性与熵对数修正的关系。

Result: 研究发现领头Bekenstein-Hawking熵探测视界面积之和,而对数修正项探测视界面积的乘积。对于Reissner-Nordström和Kerr-Newman黑洞,当经典面积乘积与质量无关时,熵的对数修正自动继承这种普适性。然而Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter黑洞提供了关键的三视界反例:面积乘积规则仍然成立,但对于物理视界,质量独立性通常会丢失。这表明统计面积乘积选择与经典普适性是两个不同的性质。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了微观熵计数与经典多视界几何之间存在结构性联系,这种联系在领头面积定律层面是不可见的。对数修正项编码了关于全局视界结构的独特信息,证明统计面积乘积选择规则与经典几何的质量独立性普适性是两个可以分离的不同性质。这为理解黑洞熵的微观起源和多视界系统的量子性质提供了新的视角,表明对数修正在连接微观统计与宏观几何方面具有重要作用。

Abstract: Black-hole entropy and multi-horizon area-product relations represent two apparently distinct manifestations of universality, arising respectively from microscopic statistics and classical geometry. We show that these structures are unexpectedly connected. Extending a minimum-assumptions statistical discretization to multiple Killing horizons, with distinct horizon sectors treated as statistically independent, we derive a general area-product rule. In particular, while the leading Bekenstein-Hawking entropy probes the sum of the horizon areas, the logarithmic correction probes their product. Consequently, whenever the classical area product is mass independent, the logarithmic entropy automatically inherits this universality. We demonstrate this for Reissner-Nordström and, under an area-based extension to rotating horizons, Kerr-Newman black holes. However, Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter provides a crucial three-horizon counterexample, for which the area-product rule survives while mass independence is generically lost for the physical horizons. Thus, statistical area-product selection and classical universality are distinct properties. Our results uncover a structural connection between microscopic entropy counting and classical multi-horizon geometry that is invisible at the level of the leading area law, suggesting that logarithmic corrections encode distinctive information about the global horizon structure.

</details>


### [127] [Potential-space geometry and scalar rigidity of the Plebański-Demiański ansatz without a cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26391)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究零宇宙常数下共形Carter类的稳态轴对称Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论,利用广义Ernst方法分析势空间。研究建立了Plebański-Demiański主坐标与Weyl数据的完整局部对应关系,得到了重构方程,并证明在所研究的二次Stäckel类中,不存在具有普通dilaton场的局部非常数解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton(EMD)理论框架下,需要系统地分析共形Carter类中的稳态轴对称时空解的存在性与性质。特别是需要明确Plebański-Demiański坐标系统与Weyl数据之间的对应关系,并确定在特定约束条件下(如二次Stäckel标量1-形式)理论允许的解空间结构。

Method: 采用广义Ernst方法在势空间上展开分析。首先建立Plebański-Demiański主坐标与Weyl数据(包括电势、磁势、扭曲势以及1-形式A、B、C)之间的完整局部对应。在无源Weyl约化中,通过要求不变面积密度为调和函数来选取Λ=0扇区,并利用主手征分裂分离电磁失配部分和加速度贡献部分。然后推导该设置下一般局部EMD构型的Einstein重构方程。在物理Carter域中引入双线性因子约束,并假设精确二次Stäckel标量1-形式,通过精确性条件与重构方程的一致性分析来限制解空间。

Result: 建立了Plebański-Demiański主坐标与Weyl数据的完整局部对应关系,覆盖了电磁和扭曲势以及相关1-形式。得到了该设置下一般局部EMD构型的Einstein重构方程。在物理Carter域中,双线性因子Ω=1-acc pq强制C=0和B_-=0。当固定非零αo时,标量场方程进一步排除了带电PD库仑型1-形式。假设精确二次Stäckel标量1-形式后,精确性条件与重构方程将系统限制到单一对齐分支,但与完整势方程的一致性要求对任意光滑Carter结构函数移除了该分支。

Conclusion: 在所研究的二次Stäckel类中,不存在具有普通dilaton场的局部非常数解。这一结论不适用于虚标量场、更高次数的共形因子或非Stäckel 1-形式情形。该结果对理解Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论中稳态轴对称解的结构具有重要意义,明确了在特定对称性和场配置假设下理论的严格约束。

Abstract: We analyze stationary, axisymmetric Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory with zero cosmological constant in the conformal Carter class, employing a generalized Ernst approach on the space of potentials. We begin by establishing a complete local correspondence between Plebański-Demiański principal coordinates and Weyl data, covering the electric, magnetic, and twist potentials as well as the 1-forms \(A\), \(B\), and \(C\). In the source-free Weyl reduction, requiring the invariant area density to be harmonic singles out the \(Λ=0\) sector, and the principal chiral splitting isolates both electromagnetic misalignment and the part due to acceleration. Next, we obtain the Einstein reconstruction equations for general local EMD configurations in this setting. In a physical Carter domain with an ordinary scalar, the bilinear factor \(\Om=1-\acc pq\) enforces \(C=0\) and \(B_-=0\). With fixed \(\alphao\neq0\), the scalar field equation further rules out the charged PD Coulomb-type 1-form. Lastly, assuming an exact quadratic Stäckel scalar 1-form, the conditions of exactness together with reconstruction restrict the system to a single aligned branch, but consistency with the full potential equations removes this branch for arbitrary smooth Carter structure functions. Consequently, within the quadratic Stäckel class studied here, there is no local nonconstant solution with an ordinary dilaton. This conclusion does not extend to phantom scalars, conformal factors of higher degree, or non-Stäckel 1-forms.

</details>


### [128] [Photon sphere for a dyonic black hole corresponding to $A_2$ Toda chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26421)
*Vladimir D. Ivashchuk,Evgeny E. Trubach*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含一个标量场和一个2-形式的四维引力模型框架下，探索具有电荷Q₁和磁荷Q₂的膨胀子dyon黑洞的光学性质，特别是零测地线的轨道特征和黑洞阴影现象，该解与A₂ Toda链相关联。

Method: 通过分析五阶多项式主方程来研究光子球半径R₀，该方程控制零测地线的圆形轨道。利用膨胀子耦合常数λ² = 3/2的固定值，研究圆形零测地线的稳定性，并通过计算临界冲击参数来分析黑洞阴影的几何特征。

Result: 证明了控制光子球半径R₀的五阶多项式方程存在唯一解，且该解满足R₀ > 2μ（大于引力半径）。确认了圆形零测地线是不稳定的。建立了黑洞阴影角与临界冲击参数之间的明确关系表达式。

Conclusion: 该膨胀子dyon黑洞具有独特的光学性质：光子球存在且唯一确定，但其上的圆形零测地线不稳定，这影响了黑洞阴影的观测特征。研究揭示了在与A₂ Toda链相关的四维引力模型中，带电黑洞的光子轨道和阴影特性，为理解复杂引力系统中的光学现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a dilatonic dyon black hole solution with a gravitational radius of $2 μ$ and two charges,
  $Q_1$ (electric) and $Q_2$ (magnetic), within the framework of a four-dimensional gravitational model comprising one scalar field and one 2-form. The dilaton coupling constant $λ$ is fixed by $λ^2 = \frac{3}{2}$.
  This solution is related to the $A_2$ Toda chain. The circular orbits of null geodesics are explored, and the fifth-order polynomial master equation governing the photon sphere radius $R_0$ is analyzed. It is shown to admit a unique solution satisfying $R_0 > 2 μ$. The circular null geodesics are proven to be unstable. Finally, the black hole shadow is examined, yielding relations for the shadow angle and the critical impact parameter.

</details>


### [129] [Stochastic motion of massless particles near event horizons in Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26475)
*Yinhan Huang,Lezhen Miao,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström时空中无质量粒子的随机径向运动，使用随机版本的内向径向零测地线方程，证明了即使在量子效应的随机扰动下，粒子在有限坐标时间内进入黑洞的概率仍为零，表明量子随机扰动保留了经典黑洞的某些性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典广义相对论中，径向运动的无质量粒子在黑洞时空外部区域需要无限坐标时间才能到达事件视界。本研究旨在探讨当考虑量子效应的随机扰动时，这一经典黑洞性质是否仍然保持，即研究随机效应对黑洞视界附近粒子运动行为的影响。

Method: 采用两种随机版本的内向径向零测地线方程来描述无质量粒子在Schwarzschild黑洞和Reissner-Nordström黑洞时空中的随机径向运动。通过随机微分方程框架，将量子效应的随机扰动引入经典测地线方程，分析粒子到达事件视界的概率分布。

Result: 研究表明，在两种黑洞时空（Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström）中，径向运动的无质量粒子在有限坐标时间内进入黑洞的概率保持为零。这一结果与经典广义相对论中的预测一致，即使在考虑随机扰动的情况下。

Conclusion: 量子效应的随机扰动保留了经典黑洞的某些基本性质，特别是粒子到达事件视界所需的无限坐标时间这一特征。这表明在坐标时间框架下，即使引入随机量子修正，黑洞视界的某些经典几何性质仍然具有鲁棒性，为理解量子效应与黑洞经典性质的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In classical general relativity, it is well known that radially moving massless particles in the exterior region take infinite coordinate time to reach the event horizon in black hole spacetimes. In this short paper, we use two stochastic versions of ingoing radial null geodesic equation to study the stochastic radial motion of massless particles in Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström spacetimes. We show that the probability that a radially moving massless particle enters the black hole in finite coordinate time remains zero. This indicates the stochastic perturbations of quantum effects preserve certain properties of classical black holes.

</details>


### [130] [Regular Cosmologies: Three Distinct Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26566)
*Sergio Bravo Medina,Marek Nowakowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究将正则黑洞的无奇点核心思想应用于早期宇宙学，通过三种不同方法（牛顿方法、在哈勃半径处评估的运行真空和基于Misner-Sharp质量的准局域方法）处理位置依赖的宇宙学项，发现单调递减的核心密度无法产生跨越w=-1的状态方程，因此无法解释DESI观测到的幽灵行为，且单一正则化尺度无法同时作为暴胀和暗能量尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则黑洞通过用德西特核心替代中心奇点来避免奇点问题。本文的动机是探索将这一思想应用于早期宇宙时会产生什么样的宇宙学图景，特别是研究正则度规定义的位置依赖宇宙学项如何转化到膨胀宇宙中，以及这种方法能否解释现代宇宙学观测（如DESI结果）。

Method: 采用三种不同的处理方法将正则黑洞的有效宇宙学项Λ_eff(r)=8πGρ_MS(r)应用到膨胀宇宙：（1）基于McCrea和Milne的牛顿方法；（2）在哈勃半径处评估的运行真空方法；（3）基于Misner-Sharp质量的准局域方法。将这三种方法分别应用于六种正则黑洞度规，对Hayward度规的牛顿情况得到弗里德曼方程的闭式解，对其他情况通过标准特殊函数或数值方法求解，并与DESI DR2观测结果进行定量比较。

Result: 牛顿方法下的宇宙学演化描述了一个从德西特相开始并以尘埃主导结束的宇宙（即暴胀模型而非暗能量模型）。在所有三种处理方法中，单调递减的核心密度产生的状态方程永远不会跨越w=-1，因此DESI观测到的幽灵行为需要正则核心不具备的密度壳层结构。三种方法在德西特核心阶段一致，但在晚期时间演化上存在差异，通过与DESI DR2结果的定量比较量化了这些差异。

Conclusion: 将正则黑洞的无奇点思想应用于宇宙学产生了从德西特相到尘埃主导的演化模型，但单调递减的核心密度无法产生跨越w=-1的幽灵行为，因此无法解释DESI的最新观测结果。更重要的是，研究指出单一的正则化长度尺度不能同时充当暴胀尺度和暗能量尺度，这对正则宇宙学模型的构建提出了基本限制。三种不同的处理方法虽然在早期演化上一致，但在晚期宇宙学行为上存在可观测的差异。

Abstract: Regular black holes avoid the central singularity by replacing it with a de Sitter core. We ask what cosmology follows if the same idea is brought to the early universe. Regular metrics define a position-dependent cosmological term, $Λ_{\rm eff}(r)=8πG ρ_{\rm MS}(r)$, and there is no unique way to carry it over to the expanding universe. We follow three different prescriptions: a Newtonian one following McCrea and Milne, a running vacuum evaluated at Hubble radius and a quasi-local one based on Misner-Sharp mass. We apply each of them to six regular black hole metrics and explore their consequences. In the Newtonian case the Friedmann equations are solved in closed form for the Hayward metric, and in terms of standard special functions or numerically for the others, and describe a universe that starts in a de Sitter phase and ends as dust (i.e. an inflationary model instead of dark energy). We find that in all three prescriptions a monotonically decreasing core density gives an equation of state that never crosses $w=-1$, and thus the phantom behaviour suggested by DESI would require a density shell which a regular core does not have. The three prescriptions agree on the de Sitter core but differ at late times, and we quantify the difference against the DESI DR2 results. We also point out that a single regularisation length cannot serve as both the inflationary and the dark-energy scale.

</details>


### [131] [Radial quantization of the Schwarzschild geometry: relational observables, evaporation, and remnant state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26575)
*David Brizuela,Leonardo Chataignier,Mikel López-Escondrillas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild几何的正则量子化，采用径向变量标记的叶层结构，构建了描述黑洞内外区域的物理希尔伯特空间。通过半经典智能态分析量子效应，发现视界位置发生偏移、表面引力增强，并获得对数修正的Bekenstein-Hawking熵，提出了可能阻止黑洞完全蒸发的稳定残余态机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑洞理论在Killing视界处存在奇异性问题，且标准量子化方法难以同时描述黑洞的内外区域。为了研究黑洞的量子效应及其对几何结构、热力学性质的影响，特别是探索黑洞蒸发终态和可能的稳定残余，需要建立一个在视界处保持正则的量子化框架。

Method: 采用基于径向变量叶层结构的minisuperspace描述对Schwarzschild几何进行正则量子化，该方法在Killing视界处保持正则性。构建满足径向约束方程的物理希尔伯特空间，定义规范不变的量子关系观测量。构造饱和Robertson-Schrödinger不确定关系的半经典智能态族，用于黑洞质量和渐近Killing范数，并保持小的相对涨落。通过这些态中关系观测量的期望值分析量子修正的时空几何。

Result: 量子效应导致视界位置根据态的关联性发生偏移。静态观测者的固有加速度和视界表面引力相比相同视界面积的经典Schwarzschild黑洞均有增强。利用表面引力与温度的关系，得到黑洞热力学性质的修正，特别是获得对数修正的Bekenstein-Hawking熵。定义了一个最小能量态，可解释为具有有限视界半径的稳定残余。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了在视界处正则的Schwarzschild黑洞量子化理论框架，揭示了量子效应对黑洞几何和热力学性质的重要影响，包括视界偏移、表面引力增强和熵的对数修正。最小能量稳定残余态的存在提供了一种可能阻止黑洞完全蒸发的机制，为理解黑洞量子性质和信息悖论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We consider the canonical quantization of the Schwarzschild geometry in a minisuperspace description based on a foliation with leaves labeled by the radial variable. This formulation remains regular across the Killing horizon. In this way, the resulting quantum theory admits a physical Hilbert space of states that solve the radial constraint equation and simultaneously describe the exterior and interior regions of the black hole. We discuss the construction of quantum relational observables, which act as gauge-invariant symmetric operators on the physical Hilbert space. In order to analyze quantum effects on the geometry, we construct a family of semiclassical intelligent states that saturate the Robertson-Schrödinger uncertainty relation for both the black-hole mass and the asymptotic Killing norm, while exhibiting small relative fluctuations. The expectation values of relational observables in these states admit a clear geometric interpretation, and they lead to an effective, quantum-corrected spacetime. In particular, we find that the position of the horizon may be shifted, depending on the correlation of the state. In addition, both the proper acceleration of a static observer and the horizon surface gravity are enhanced with respect to a classical Schwarzschild black hole with the same horizon area. Using the relation between surface gravity and temperature, we then obtain corrections to the thermodynamic properties of the black hole, leading to a logarithmically corrected Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. Finally, we discuss the definition of a state of minimal energy that could be interpreted as a stable remnant characterized by a finite horizon radius, suggesting a possible mechanism for preventing the complete evaporation of the black hole.

</details>
